We evaluated our pipeline on instances across 20 MiniZinc competition problems, finding that algorithm selection achieves a 39.5% average problem-weighted win rate against a one-shot Gurobi baseline, more than doubling the best single configuration (19.3%). Configuration and feature ablation boost the performance further to 44.0%, demonstrating that LLM-based semantic generation enables effective automated structure extraction and feature extraction for constraint optimization.

</details>


### [237] [LongEarth-R1: Benchmarking and Aligning Vision-Language Models for Long-Horizon Earth Observation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13344)
*Yupan Ding,Jing Xiao,Zhenyuan Zhang,Chaofeng Chen,Liang Liao,Gui-Song Xia,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了LongEarth-Bench基准，包含约12万问答样本和11.7万张独特图像，支持最长30帧的长时序遥感推理；并提出了LongEarth及LongEarth-R1两个视觉语言模型，通过监督微调与组相对策略优化（GRPO）在12项长序列任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉语言模型主要聚焦于孤立图像、图像对或短序列，无法有效处理长时序地理演化推理，缺乏可靠的长时序帧级与区域级定位能力。

Method: 1) 构建LongEarth-Bench基准，含约120k问答样本，序列平均15.14帧，最长30帧，覆盖12项任务（演化总结、空间推理、异常识别、逻辑预测），并提供30k带结构化推理轨迹的子集；2) 提出LongEarth模型，采用带显式序列标识符和结构化思维链监督的监督微调；3) 提出LongEarth-R1，应用组相对策略优化（GRPO），引入格式奖励、时间奖励和空间奖励。

Result: LongEarth-R1在所有12项长序列任务上取得最佳结果，同时在标准遥感基准上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 长时序遥感理解需要专门的基准与训练策略，结构化思维链监督结合带时间与空间奖励的GRPO优化，能够有效提升长时序推理能力并保持通用遥感任务性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon Earth observation reasoning requires models to organize multi-stage geographic evolution, localize spatial changes, detect temporal anomalies, and infer future from extended image sequences. However, existing remote sensing vision-language models mainly focus on isolated images, image pairs, or short sequences, limiting reliable grounding in the relevant frames and regions. We introduce LongEarth-Bench, a benchmark containing approximately 120k question-answering samples derived from 117k unique images. Its sequences average 15.14 frames and extend to 30 frames, covering 12 tasks across evolution summarization, spatial reasoning, anomaly identification, and logical prediction. A 30k-sample subset further provides structured reasoning traces linking key frames and changed regions to final answers. We develop LongEarth through supervised fine-tuning with explicit sequence identifiers and structured chain-of-thought supervision. Building on LongEarth, LongEarth-R1 applies group relative policy optimization with format, temporal, and spatial rewards. LongEarth-R1 achieves the best results on all 12 long-sequence tasks while remaining competitive on standard remote sensing benchmarks.

</details>


### [238] [Rules or Character? Scaling Laws for AI Safety Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13345)
*Satoshi Takahashi,Nobuji Kouno,Masaaki Komatsu,Ryuji Hamamoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个比较静态模型，研究AI安全系统中性格塑造（如RLHF）与规则执行（如输出过滤器）之间的最优资源分配α随部署规模T的变化趋势。通过闭式解的期望风险分析和蒙特卡洛模拟的尾部风险（CVaR）分析，发现最优α*随规模增大而向性格塑造方向移动（Δα*从+0.01到+0.21不等），且基准性格脆弱率是关键主导参数（对α*的影响达0.50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全系统通常结合训练时的性格塑造与推理时的规则执行两种方式，但缺乏正式的数学分析来理解这两者在部署规模扩大时应如何调整最优平衡点。

Method: 构建一个参数化安全设计的比较静态模型，用α∈[0,1]表示两种方法间的资源分配；纳入规模相关的过滤器退化、共模故障和性格脆弱性；在乘法Pareto损害模型下推导期望危害的闭式解，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行CVaR尾部风险分析；在乐观、中度和悲观三种场景下求解最优α*。

Result: 三种场景下最优α*均为内部解或位于规则执行边界，且随部署规模T增大而向性格塑造方向偏移（Δα*范围+0.01至+0.21）。基准性格脆弱率p^(0)_frag是主导参数，对α*的影响达0.50，远超尾部严重性、过滤器质量或共模故障概率的影响。在大规模T下，CVaR最优与期望危害最优趋于收敛。

Conclusion: 安全架构的决策对部署规模本身的依赖并不显著，关键取决于性格塑造在分布偏移条件下的可靠性。该发现为实际安全系统设计提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) safety systems combine character shaping (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback [RLHF], Constitutional AI), which modifies behavioral distributions at training time, with rule enforcement (e.g., output filters, safety classifiers), which blocks harmful outputs at inference time, yet little formal analysis exists on how their optimal balance should change as deployment scales increase. We introduce a stylized comparative-statics model that parameterizes safety design as a resource allocation alpha in [0,1] between these two approaches, incorporating scale-dependent filter degradation, common-mode failures, and character fragility -- the risk that shaped behavior degrades or collapses under novel conditions. Under a multiplicative Pareto damage model, we derive closed-form expected harm and supplement it with tail-risk (CVaR) analysis via Monte Carlo simulation. Across three scenarios (optimistic, moderate, pessimistic), the optimal alpha* is interior or at the rules-only boundary and shifts weakly toward character shaping as deployment scale T grows, from negligible (Delta alpha* = +0.01) to pronounced (Delta alpha* = +0.21) depending on scenario. The dominant parameter is the baseline character fragility rate p^(0)_frag, which shifts alpha* by 0.50 across its range -- far exceeding the effect of tail severity, filter quality, or common-mode failure probability. CVaR and expected-harm optima converge at large T. These results suggest that safety architecture decisions depend less on deployment scale per se than on the reliability of character shaping under distributional shift.

</details>


### [239] [TopoIntent: Compiling Security Intent into Executable, Compliance-Checked Network Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13389)
*Xiaokang Qu,Jianliang Ma,Zao Fan,Tianshu Chu,Tianlong Fan,Linyuan Lü*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoIntent是一个从自然语言安全意图编译为可执行、合规检查的网络拓扑的系统。它通过模式契约约束生成，利用密集向量检索从模板库获取参考架构，采用分阶段融合进行意图-模板对齐与安全补全，生成的拓扑对标CIS v8.1.2可见于拓扑层的安全控制项，并通过additive schema-preserving edits修复结构缺口，最后导出为Mininet脚本及内核级iptables ACL，支持可执行的可达性和允许/拒绝测试。在自建评估集上，additive repair将CIS满意度从0.78提升至1.00，单轮反馈将ACL策略通过率从0.78提升至0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NetOps自动化工具多在安全拓扑设计定型之后运行，无法从未指定的自然语言需求中生成结构化安全拓扑。企业安全拓扑设计需要将业务意图、监管要求和风险假设转化为区域、边界设备、区域间路径及访问控制策略，而缺乏从模糊需求到结构化拓扑的自动生成支持。

Method: TopoIntent使用schema contract约束生成过程；通过dense-vector search从精选模板库检索参考架构；应用staged fusion实现意图-模板对齐与安全补全；生成拓扑与CIS Controls v8.1.2中拓扑层可见的安全防护项进行合规检查，未解决案例标记为人工审查；结构缺口通过additive schema-preserving edits修复；最终导出为Mininet脚本并附带内核级iptables ACL，实现可执行验证。

Result: 在金融和政府场景的held-out测试集上，additive repair使拓扑可见的CIS满足度从0.78提升到1.00，且平均少于1.5轮完成；单轮反馈将post-ACL策略通过率从0.78提升至0.88。评估集包含22个检索模板和44个合成意图（五个场景），以及7个保留模板和14个来自金融与政府场景的held-out意图。

Conclusion: TopoIntent证明从自然语言安全意图到合规、可执行网络拓扑的端到端编译是可行的，通过模式契约、模板检索、分阶段融合和增量修复的结合，能够高效地生成满足CIS基准的拓扑，并支持可执行验证。该系统为安全拓扑的自动化生成提供了可行的范式。

Abstract: Enterprise security topology design requires translating business intent, regulatory requirements, and risk assumptions into zones, boundary devices, inter-zone paths, and access-control policies. Existing NetOps automation tools mainly operate after this design is fixed, providing limited support for generating structured security topologies from underspecified natural-language requirements. We present TopoIntent, a system that compiles security intent into executable, compliance-checked network topologies. It uses a schema contract to constrain generation, retrieves reference architectures from a curated template library via dense-vector search, and applies staged fusion for intent-template alignment and security completion. The generated topology is checked against CIS Controls v8.1.2 safeguards visible at the topology layer, while unresolved cases are marked for manual review. Structural gaps are repaired through additive schema-preserving edits. The final topology is exported to Mininet scripts with kernel-level iptables ACLs, enabling executable reachability and allow/deny tests. Because no public benchmark exists for this requirement-to-topology task, we construct an evaluation set from reference security architecture diagrams. The retrieval set contains 22 templates and 44 synthetic intents across five scenarios, while the held-out set contains 7 templates and 14 intents from finance and government scenarios excluded from retrieval. On the held-out set, additive repair improves topology-visible CIS satisfaction from 0.78 to 1.00 in fewer than 1.5 rounds on average, and one feedback round raises the post-ACL policy pass rate from 0.78 to 0.88.

</details>


### [240] [Jointly Predicting Courses and Grades Using a Transformer-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13409)
*Paul Savala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE（TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation）模型，通过同时预测学生下学期的选课集合和对应成绩，克服了传统学习分析模型将学业历史视为简单序列而忽略学期内课程并行性的局限。在十年机构数据上训练后，联合预测模型比仅预测成绩的相同架构降低近50%的平均绝对误差，并优于LSTM序列模型和图神经网络方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习分析中的预测模型将学生的学业历史视为简单序列，忽略了同一学期内多门课程并行修读的性质，导致对课业负担重或课程难度大的学生预测不准确。作者旨在通过捕捉课程并发性来提升成绩预测精度。

Method: TRACE采用Transformer架构，以学期为单位对课程进行编码以捕获课程并发效应；设计了一种结合课程集合预测与成绩预测的新型损失函数，实现学期选课与成绩的联合预测。模型还支持自然融入学生属性数据。

Result: 在十年机构数据上训练后，联合预测模型相比仅预测成绩的相同架构将平均绝对误差降低近50%；同时优于传统的LSTM序列模型和图神经网络方法。

Conclusion: 现代神经架构可用于构建可解释的学习分析模型，通过预测课程修读等关键技巧显著提升预测性能；模型可通过重训练和重新校准适应新机构，并有望整合到高等教育机构的早期检测系统中。

Abstract: Existing predictive models in learning analytics often treat student academic history as a simple sequence, overlooking the concurrent nature of courses taken within a semester. This simplification can lead to inaccurate performance predictions, particularly for students with heavy or challenging course loads. This paper introduces a TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation (TRACE) that addresses this limitation by jointly predicting both the set of courses a student will take and their corresponding grades for an upcoming semester. Our approach encodes courses on a per-semester basis to capture the effects of course concurrency and utilizes a novel loss function combining course-set prediction with grade prediction. We demonstrate that predicting courses taken in addition to the grades in those courses leads to significant improvements in prediction quality. Trained on ten years of institutional data, our joint prediction model reduces mean absolute error by nearly 50% compared to an identical architecture that predicts grades alone. The model also outperforms traditional LSTM-based sequential models, as well as graph neural network-based approaches, and offers natural ways to incorporate student attribute data. This work demonstrates the utility of modern neural architectures for creating interpretable models that can be adapted to new institutions via retraining and recalibration, as well as the importance of key techniques, such as predicting courses taken during training. We discuss how this model could be incorporated into early detection systems at institutions of higher education.

</details>


### [241] [Who Speaks Matters: Authority-Aware Multi-View RAG over Italian Parliamentary Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13410)
*Mirko Tritella,Riccardo Pozzi,Matteo Palmonari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ParliamentRAG，一个面向意大利众议院议会记录的检索增强生成系统，通过主题依赖的发言人权威模型，联合解决RAG应用于议会文本时的三大风险：高频发言人主导、无法按主题专长加权、以及敏感政治文本中的引文误归属问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 议会记录是民主审议的主要载体，但其体量庞大且碎片化，使公民、记者和研究者难以进行多视角访问。将RAG应用到议会转录文本会引入三个具体风险：最频繁发言者主导检索结果、无法根据主题专长对发言人加权、以及在政治敏感文本中产生引文错误归属。

Method: 提出ParliamentRAG系统，核心贡献是主题依赖的权威模型，该模型根据当前查询估计每位发言人的权威度，结合职业、教育背景、既往发言等可解释组件。系统流程为：按用户查询检索相关发言片段，跨议会团体识别主题相关专家，生成综合各视角的摘要并附支持性引文。

Result: 在15个政策主题上与Google NotebookLM对比评估，采用自动化指标和六位领域专家的盲A/B人工评估两级协议。结果显示：跨政治团体覆盖率更高（0.97 vs 0.95）、引文忠实度完美（1.00 vs 0.95）、源相关维度上专家偏好更强；而NotebookLM在散文取向维度上更强。

Conclusion: ParliamentRAG在源相关质量和引文可靠性方面优于NotebookLM，证实了主题依赖权威模型对议会多视角检索增强生成的有效性，但散文生成的流畅性方面仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [242] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对七个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上进行了系统评估，提出了一种新框架，利用基于规则的评价指标刻画运行内行为（解决方案构思、执行、反馈控制），并通过受控对比评估经验复用。结果显示当前智能体更像"工程优化器"而非"全自主研究者"：能提出和实施实用方案，但跨运行表现波动大、强方案多为对现有技术的适配组合、真正的方法论创新仍稀缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注最终分数，无法揭示性能提升或损失发生在何处，也无法判断累积经验是否改善后续决策。为全面理解自主智能体在长时程实验中的能力现状，需要超越端到端评分的新型评估框架。

Method: 提出新评估框架，包含两层设计：(1) 基于规则的指标从解决方案构思（Solution Framing）、执行（Execution）和反馈控制（Feedback Control）三个维度刻画智能体在运行内的行为；(2) 通过受控对比实验评估经验在任务内部和跨任务间的复用效果。在36个长时程任务上系统评估七个前沿模型。

Result: 当前智能体表现类似工程优化器：能制定并实施实用方案，但跨运行性能波动显著；最强解决方案主要是对既有技术的改编或组合，真正的方法论创新很少。进一步分析发现：相似最终结果背后可能存在不同过程瓶颈；经验复用既可能帮助也可能误导后续决策；系统（harness）设计会影响性能稳定性。

Conclusion: 这些发现为改进模型训练、推理时策略、经验管理和系统设计提供了具体方向，表明当前自主智能体距离完全自主的科研能力仍有一定差距。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [243] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用小型语言模型（SLMs）在边缘计算环境下支持认知具身智能体架构（CEAA）中的"思考"与"记忆"两个核心过程，并在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上开发评估了一个边缘虚拟智能体网关系统，实验证明SLM驱动具备低延迟和高上下文感知能力，可支撑沉浸式虚拟世界中的具身智能体部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟代理需要在复杂虚拟世界和Metaverse中持续、自适应和上下文感知地运行，但实现具有认知能力的代理在概念和技术上都具有挑战性。现有架构方案缺乏实现导向的组件设计，且考虑到边缘计算与生成式AI语言模型的最新进展，探索SLM在边缘端支撑CEAA组件的可行性与性能表现成为重要的研究方向。

Method: 基于CEAA框架，聚焦"Think"和"Memory"两个核心过程，设计并实现了一个边缘虚拟代理网关系统。该系统部署在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX平台上，使用不同尺寸的Qwen2.5模型作为SLM后端，通过一系列模拟实验评估系统的路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟指标。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的SLM驱动原型代理系统能够部分实现CEAA的选定过程，在路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟方面均取得了可行表现，展示了SLM在边缘设备上支撑具身智能体认知"大脑"高效、上下文相关运行的潜力。

Conclusion: SLM结合边缘计算能够有效支持认知具身智能体架构中的关键认知过程，为在沉浸式虚拟世界中部署轻量、高效且具备上下文感知能力的具身智能代理提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [244] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一AI就绪度等级（AIRL）九级量表及RAIL专家小组分类器，使AI技术成熟度可从自然语言描述自动判定，避免单一LLM分类器的高估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI技术成熟度对投资决策、项目管理和政策监测至关重要，但现有就绪度框架异构且难以自动应用：TRL缺乏AI特异性门控标准，MLTRL依赖内部过程产物，AI/数据就绪度维度模型量表难以直接比较。

Method: 提出两重贡献：（1）统一三种框架为AIRL九级有序量表，基于环境证据阶梯，辅以维度上限（涵盖规格、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、专家知识、算法成熟度）、通用性锚定规则和明确的赋值纪律，使就绪度等级仅凭工作自然语言描述即可判定；（2）提出RAIL——由证据代理和六个独立维度代理组成的LLM专家小组分类器，每个代理任务范围窄化，通过确定性最小规则聚合，由首席专家在非对称权限下审查，可确认或下调但绝不上调超出上限。

Result: 该方法在多个研究工作的分析中进行了测试，显示出结果一致性，并避免了单一LLM分类器的高估问题。

Conclusion: AIRL与RAIL提供了可自动化的AI技术成熟度评估方案，通过多专家分工与上限约束机制提升了评估的可靠性和判别力。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [245] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学（UFSC）人工智能学术联盟（LIA）的组织框架，该框架采用以学生为中心、基于项目的方法，将教学、科研和大学拓展三大支柱整合起来，通过民主治理、协作学习和动态项目管理培养技术与横向能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育和加强大学与社会融合的重要机制，但如何在统一的组织结构中集成教学、科研和大学拓展三大支柱仍缺乏可复制的实践框架。

Method: 提出了一个结合民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织的组织框架，并通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库和具有社会影响的人工智能应用等代表性项目加以验证。

Result: 该框架成功在统一组织结构中开展了多样化的教育、科学和拓展活动，促进了领导力、科研成果、社区参与和知识保存，表明该框架灵活且可复制。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为工程和计算机教育中整合大学三大支柱提供了可复制的模型，可为学术联盟及类似学生组织提供实践指导。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [246] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角系统性地研究了世界模型（World Models），提出了"因果世界模型"（Causal World Models, CWMs）的形式化定义，强调世界模型不应仅具备生成能力，还应捕捉实体属性、实体间及实体与环境间的因果关系，以支持因果推理和智能决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被认为是智能体预测、规划和在分布外行动的基础，但现有世界模型研究多聚焦于生成能力，缺乏对因果结构和因果表征的系统性关注。作者认为，真正有用的世界模型必须超越生成能力，捕捉并利用环境中的因果机制。

Method: 作者从因果视角出发，将世界模型概念与因果表征学习、以对象为中心的学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策制定等已有工作相连接，提出CWMs的形式化定义，并从可辨识性（identifiability）角度分析世界模型各组成部分能否从数据中恢复及其等价程度。

Result: 本文提供了因果世界模型的正式框架定义，明确了世界模型应捕获的因果结构和表征层次，并系统梳理了各组件在何种条件下可被辨识以及其等价性类别。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型建立在支持因果推理和知情决策的表征与结构之上，本文为世界模型的研究提供了理论基石，弥合了生成模型与因果建模之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [247] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARC是一个开源的多智能体推理与协调框架，用确定性的多智能体编排替代临床推理中的单体LLM提示词方法，支持提取、推理、答案生成和评估的角色分工，并引入Decomposer模块自动生成任务特定提示词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单体LLM提示词在临床推理中缺乏可解释性和可追溯性，难以定位失败环节，且提示词工程需要大量人工工作，对临床领域专家不友好。

Method: 采用多智能体编排，包括提取、推理、答案生成和评估四个角色分工的智能体，通过显式上下文传递和可追踪的中间输出实现分阶段失败归因；引入Decomposer模块从自然语言描述自动生成任务特定的智能体提示词；支持API和本地CPU兼容部署，通过YAML完全配置，无需代码修改。

Result: 框架具有模型无关性、可解释性和对无编程经验的临床领域专家的可访问性，已在GitHub上开源发布。

Conclusion: MARC通过确定性的多智能体编排和自动化提示词生成，为临床推理提供了一种更透明、可追溯且面向领域专家开放的替代方案。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [248] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AlayaWorld的改进版本，其核心思想是让条件信号在潜在表征和时间结构上尽可能与生成内容匹配，通过六项具体修改（包括用流式3D点缓存渲染器替代深度扭曲空间记忆、统一条件与生成的VAE潜在空间等）显著改进了条件信号的表示与集成方式，同时保持骨干架构、分块自回归生成方案和训练数据不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前版本中条件信号与生成内容在潜在表征和时间结构上存在不一致，导致条件信息难以被高效利用。作者希望建立一条指导原则：条件信号应在潜在表征和时间结构上尽可能接近生成内容，从而提升视频生成的质量与条件控制精度。

Method: 作者提出六项关键修改：(1) 用运动感知的潜在条件替代静态帧图像条件；(2) 将重渲染的空间记忆作为连续序列进行因果编码；(3) 在像素空间中对齐时间记忆窗口；(4) 采用硬记忆丢弃（直接移除记忆token而非置零）；(5) 统一训练与推理阶段的VAE编解码协议；(6) 移除相机AdaLN分支，视点控制完全由重渲染的空间条件提供。同时将深度扭曲空间记忆替换为流式3D点缓存渲染器。

Result: 新版本在保持骨干架构、分块自回归生成方案和训练数据不变的前提下，通过重新设计条件信号的表示与集成方式，使条件信号在潜在表征和时间结构上与生成内容高度一致，取得了系统性改进。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了"条件信号应与生成内容在潜在空间和时间结构上对齐"这一原则的有效性，为视频生成模型中条件信号的设计提供了新的方向，并展示了通过统一条件与生成侧的表征协议来提升生成质量与可控性的可行路径。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [249] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuoteBench是一个基准测试，通过精确最终状态验证在56个单轮任务（源自14个事件族）中测量LLM编码代理生成Bash命令时的边界问题。研究发现匹配的执行分数无法区分命令生成错误与执行传输中的失败，而通过额外解析器重放相同回复会使成功率下降55.4-73.2个百分点；披露边界后六个配置可恢复30.4-60.7个百分点，另两个配置恢复为零或负值。部署配置会重新排序模型，因此评估命令发布代理时应报告完整配置而非仅报告匹配分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理通过接口发布Bash命令时，模型输出可能被序列化、包装和重新解析。仅依赖匹配的执行分数无法区分命令生成阶段的错误与生成后执行阶段引入的失败，这掩盖了问题的真实来源，需要一种方法来衡量这一边界。

Method: QuoteBench设计了56个单轮任务（来自14个事件衍生族），采用精确最终状态验证，通过交叉生成合约与执行传输（刻意加入一个未转义的解析器）来测量边界。在插入点进行转义可复现每条重放回复的原始路径结果，使恢复只能来自模型改变其生成。在八个相同窗口配置中重放同一回复。

Result: 通过额外解析器重放相同回复使成功率降低55.4-73.2个百分点；披露边界后六个配置恢复30.4-60.7个百分点，两个配置恢复为零或负值。原始生成在前沿已接近饱和，边界适应才是区分模型的关键。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距-3.6点掩盖了-64.3点的损伤和+60.7点的补偿。部署配置在26个可比较模型对中重新排序，其中一个反转明确，四个处于单任务边缘。

Conclusion: 对命令发布代理的评估应报告模型配置、生成合约、执行路径、操作点和最终状态验证器，而不应将匹配分数视为模型的内在属性。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [250] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniScientist，一个端到端全模态AI科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据（图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式、图等）开展跨学科研究。系统通过感知层和3个自主智能体（构思、实验、撰写）在确定性流程中协作，并以代码方式实现新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追踪性检查。在涵盖5大学科族、36个真实数据案例上的评估显示，系统在所有案例中均完成从原始数据到编译稿件的全过程，平均论文得分6.3，且直接感知相比仅接收预计算特征的盲变体在全部7个评估维度上占优、赢得85%的对比判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统主要在文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要上推理，无法访问具有科学决定性的空间、时间、跨通道和程序性关系。工作流覆盖率不等于获得完整证据访问权，因此需要让AI科学家直接从异构原始证据出发开展研究。

Method: OmniScientist由感知层和三个自主智能体（构思、实验、撰写）组成，在确定性流水线中运行，使观测结果能贯穿研究生命周期塑造研究问题、实验决策和最终结论。系统通过代码执行想法检查、严谨性检查和声明检查，实现新颖性筛选、统计有效性、执行来源和数值可追溯性。

Result: 在36个真实数据案例（涵盖5个学科家族、4类科学证据和多模态数据）上，系统全部完成从原始数据到编译手稿的完整流程，参考推理骨干下平均论文得分6.3。与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体相比，直接感知在所有7个评估维度上均表现更优，并在85%的头对头比较中获胜。

Conclusion: 全生命周期感知对于基于证据的科学发现至关重要，OmniScientist为构建更广泛能力的AI科学家提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [251] [A Repeated-Game Framework for Incentives in Decentralized Infrastructure Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12576)
*Mustafa Qazi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种重复动态激励框架，用于刻画去中心化物理基础设施网络（DePIN）中"合规"（即全额努力、诚实提供服务）何时是激励兼容的。核心贡献是"威慑比率"Γ（偏差的私人收益与其边际检测概率的最坏情况比率），并据此给出了合规顺序激励兼容的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DePIN网络中协议方需要通过经济激励机制确保服务提供商合规运作，但现有文献缺乏对此类重复道德风险问题的系统性刻画。作者希望回答：在什么条件下，协议能够通过质押惩罚（slashing）和声誉惩罚（降级到试用层）的组合来有效威慑提供商偏离合规行为。

Method: 将协议与每个提供商之间的质量管控建模为重复道德风险问题（repeated moral-hazard problem）。合规性通过两方面强制执行：（1）对质押抵押品的罚没（slashing）；（2）在声誉阶梯上降级到试用层的折现威胁。作者定义了威慑比率Γ，并给出了合规激励兼容的充要条件：当所有有利可图的偏差都具有正检测差距，且不存在弱成本更高但失败概率更低的行为时，当且仅当即时罚没加上折现声誉损失在每一个声誉层级上至少等于Γ，合规是顺序激励兼容的。基于此条件，进一步构建了从服务原语到质押要求、奖励计划、试用规则和审计频率的协议设计问题。

Result: 主要发现：如果任何有利可图的偏差不增加相对于合规的失败概率，则二元公开结果（binary public-outcome）协议无法威慑该偏差。当所有有利可图的偏差都具有正检测差距时，给出了合规顺序激励兼容的充要条件（威慑条件：即时罚没+折现声誉惩罚 ≥ Γ）。该条件可用于制定协议设计问题，映射服务原语到具体机制参数。

Conclusion: 该研究为DePIN协议设计提供了理论基础，明确了组合使用质押罚没与声誉降级机制来确保合规的充分必要条件。核心洞见是：当偏差不增加被检测失败的概率时，仅靠二元结果无法实现威慑；而当存在正检测差距时，可通过精心设计的威慑比率条件实现激励兼容。

Abstract: We introduce a repeated dynamic incentive framework for characterizing when "compliance", or full-effort honest service provision, is incentive compatible in Decentralized Physical Infrastructure Networks (DePIN). We model quality control in this setting as a repeated moral-hazard problem between the protocol and each provider, where compliance is enforced by both slashing posted collateral and the discounted threat of demotion to probation tiers on a reputation ladder. Our main contribution is the "deterrence ratio" $Γ$, the worst-case ratio of a deviation's private gain to its marginal probability of detection. We find that if any profitable deviation does not increase the fail probability relative to compliance, then binary public-outcome protocols cannot deter it. When all profitable deviations have positive detection gaps and no weakly costlier action is less likely to fail, compliance is sequentially incentive compatible if and only if immediate slashing plus discounted reputation loss is at least $Γ$ at every reputation tier. We use this condition to formulate a protocol design problem mapping service primitives to stake requirements, reward schedules, probation rules, and audit frequency.

</details>


### [252] [Error-Aware Reverse Auction Mechanism for Large Language Model Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12719)
*Haolong Chen,Zhengyuan Xin,Liang Zhang,Lei Xue,Guangxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 提出一种基于市场的LLM路由范式EA-RAM，通过反向拍卖让提供商自报成功概率和执行成本，显式建模预测与评估的固有双误差，从集中式预测转向去中心化路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由依赖集中式任务中心预测模型性能，存在信息-风险不匹配和模型池扩大时的可扩展性瓶颈。需要一种去中心化、能够处理不可靠预测的路由机制。

Method: 提出EA-RAM（误差感知反向拍卖机制），将事前预测转移给LLM提供商，提供商通过反向拍卖竞标，提交自预测成功概率和执行成本。机制显式建模提供商预测与中心评估的固有双误差；证明贝叶斯激励兼容性和个体理性；建立中心理性的充分条件；推导显式福利损失边界；分析误差抵消、饱和链接函数稳定化和噪声平滑化三种鲁棒性效应。

Result: 在模拟和真实基准上，EA-RAM对双误差具有鲁棒性，相比集中式基线实现了更好的成本-性能帕累托前沿；提供商贡献本地信息时获得额外收益。

Conclusion: 市场驱动的去中心化路由范式在LLM路由中切实有效，能处理固有的预测噪声，优于集中式方法，且可结合本地信息进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Routing each query to a cost-effective large language model (LLM) is critical for balancing quality and cost, yet most routers rely on a centralized task center to predict model performance, creating an information-risk mismatch and a scalability bottleneck as the model pool grows. We propose a market-based routing paradigm that shifts ex-ante prediction to LLM providers via a reverse auction, where providers bid with self-predicted success probabilities and execution costs. To account for inherently noisy provider predictions and center evaluations, we introduce the \textit{\textbf{E}rror-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}everse \textbf{A}uction \textbf{M}echanism} (EA-RAM), which explicitly models this inherent Dual Error. We prove that EA-RAM is Bayesian incentive compatible and individually rational under the Dual Error, establish sufficient conditions for center rationality, and derive an explicit welfare-loss bound. We further identify robustness effects: opposite-signed errors can cancel, vanishing-tail link functions (e.g., logistic) stabilize clear-cut cases via saturation, and extra noise smooths belief maps, reducing the gains from marginal manipulation. Experiments on simulations and real-world benchmarks show that EA-RAM is robust to the Dual Error and achieves a better cost--performance Pareto frontier than centralized baselines, with additional gains when providers contribute local information, validating its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [253] [Representation in Peer Selection: A Liquid Democracy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13085)
*Davide Grossi,Grzegorz Lisowski,Georgios Papasotiropoulos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究"同行选择问题"（peer selection），引入一类基于液态民主中委托图与信任传递性的偏好剖面——"液态剖面"（liquid profiles），在此域限制下分析了既有与新提出的比例性公理的行为及其相互关系，并研究了多种投票规则（含新设计的）对这些公理的满足情况。结果表明在该新域中可获得特别强的比例性保障，为现实世界的同行选择应用（通过传递性委托）提供了前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行选择问题是一个重要的社会选择议题，例如委员会遴选等场景。已有域限制（如单峰偏好等）未必贴合现实中的委托信任结构；同时，液态民主中的委托图为偏好结构提供了自然的启发。作者希望引入一个新的偏好域来更好刻画现实同行选择场景，并探索在该域中能否实现更强的比例性保障。

Method: 作者定义了"液态剖面"这一新的审批制偏好域：每位选民指定一位受信任代理人，并审批该代理人（及其信任链）所信任的所有人。论文首先将该域与标准受限偏好剖面（如单峰、单交叉等）建立联系，研究其计算性质；随后在该域中考察既有的和新提出的比例性公理（如D'Hondt比例性、EJR、PJR等）及其相互关系；最后分析多种投票规则（包括基于液态剖面结构新设计的规则）对这些公理的满足情况。

Result: 研究发现液态剖面具有独特的结构性特征，与已知受限偏好域存在关联但又有区别；在该域中，多种比例性公理之间的关系更加清晰，且特别强的比例性保障（强于一般审批制偏好下的可达程度）是可实现的。新设计的投票规则在液态剖面下表现出优异的比例性保证。

Conclusion: 液态剖面作为一个新的偏好域，为同行选择问题提供了理论与应用两方面的价值：一方面它刻画了由传递性委托形成的真实偏好结构，另一方面在该域中可实现强的比例性保障，展示了通过传递性委托解决现实同行选择问题的潜力。

Abstract: We study the peer selection problem in which a group of peers must select a fixed-size committee from among themselves. To this end, we introduce a class of approval-based preference profiles, inspired by delegation graphs in liquid democracy and the transitivity of trust therein, which we call ``liquid profiles''. Under this new domain restriction, each voter designates a single trusted agent and, in turn, approves all those trusted by that individual. After establishing relationships to standard restricted preference profiles and examining computational properties of liquid profiles, we focus on understanding how novel and established proportionality axioms behave in this domain and how they relate to one another. Then, we study their satisfaction under various voting rules, including new ones that are directly inspired by the structure of liquid profiles. Our findings reveal that particularly strong proportionality guarantees are attainable in this new domain, showing promise for applications to real-world peer selection problems via transitive delegations.

</details>


### [254] [Power in Liquid Democracy: A Network Centrality Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13188)
*Davide Grossi,Andreas Nitsche,Georgios Papasotiropoulos,Oskar Skibski,Piotr Skowron,Tomasz Wąs*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个计算上可处理的框架，用于衡量支持传递性和可暂停委托的集体决策平台（即流动民主）中选民的权力，采用随机游走衰减中心性来刻画影响力在委托网络中的传播，并给出了公理化特征描述以及代表性参与者名单选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流动民主平台中，选民的权力如何量化是一个关键但尚未被充分解决的问题。现有方法（如PageRank）在这个场景中存在适用性局限，需要一种直观、可计算且可公理化的权力衡量框架，以支持影响力分析和参与者代表性选择。

Method: 采用随机游走衰减（Random Walk Decay）中心性来捕捉影响力在委托网络中的传播；从公理化角度论证该指标的合理性并比较其与PageRank等替代方法的优劣；并将该框架扩展为分析工具，进一步提出从公理化和算法化两个角度研究选择有影响力的代表性参与者名单（slate）的方法。

Result: 获得了随机游走衰减中心性在流动民主权力度量中的自然公理化刻画；建立了该框架向实际分析工具的扩展路径；提出了选择有影响力参与者代表名单的方法，并从公理化与算法两个维度进行了研究。

Conclusion: 随机游走衰减中心性是在流动民主语境下衡量选民权力的一种直观且可公理化论证的有效方法，优于PageRank等替代方案；该框架不仅可用于权力分布分析，还可扩展为实际工具，并支持代表性参与者名单的有效选择。

Abstract: This paper develops a computationally tractable framework for measuring voters' power in collective decision-making platforms that support transitive and suspendable delegations---commonly known as liquid democracy. We employ Random Walk Decay centrality to capture how influence propagates through delegation networks, argue for its intuitive appeal in this context and its advantages over alternatives such as PageRank, and derive a natural axiomatic characterization within the class of power metrics. Moreover, we conceptualize how the framework can be extended into a practical tool for analyzing power distributions in such platforms, and propose and study, both axiomatically and algorithmically, methods for selecting representative slates of influential participants.

</details>


### [255] [Keep, Customize, or Exit: Default Design and Token Pricing in LLM Reasoning Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13315)
*Ahmet Bugra Gundogan,Yigit Turkmen,Melih Bastopcu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个大语言模型推理服务的定价与默认推理token分配策略问题。在Stackelberg博弈框架下，服务商设定每token价格和默认推理分配量，用户可以接受默认、自定义分配或退出。作者推导出用户最优自定义分配的闭式解，刻画了服务商最优默认的三区制规则，证明了均衡的存在性，并通过五个数学与科学基准上的实验验证了模型假设与均衡分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中，推理token分配（推理预算）是影响答案准确性的关键因素，但更多的推理token意味着更高的成本和延迟。服务商需要决定如何设置价格与默认推理分配，用户则需要决定是否接受默认值或进行自定义，这在实践中存在显著的策略交互问题，但目前缺乏系统的博弈论分析。

Method: 作者将服务商与用户的交互建模为Stackelberg博弈：服务商先选择每token价格和默认推理token分配，用户随后基于效用最大化选择接受默认、自定义分配或退出。通过效用函数建模推导用户最优自定义分配的闭式解；分析给定价格下可接受默认值的集合结构；通过三区制规则刻画服务商最优默认选择；将均衡计算约化为一维价格优化问题，并证明均衡存在性。实验在五个数学和科学基准上使用两个开放权重推理模型验证准确性-成本模型。

Result: 用户的最优自定义分配具有闭式解；对于任何价格，可接受的默认集要么为空要么为紧区间。服务商的最优默认遵循三区制规则：需要较大推理分配、较小推理分配或令默认值失效。均衡计算可约化为一维价格搜索，均衡存在性得到证明。默认值仅在用户重视"避免自定义"的便利性时才影响最终实现的推理分配；否则服务提供的任何结果都实现用户的最优自定义分配。实验结果与准确性-成本模型一致，并展示了模型与任务特征如何决定均衡价格、默认值和推理分配。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM推理服务的定价与推理预算默认设置提供了完整的博弈论框架，揭示了服务商默认推理分配的影响条件与边界，并为理解推理成本、准确性与用户体验之间的权衡提供了理论依据与实证支持。

Abstract: We study a large language model (LLM) service in which a provider chooses a per-token price and a default reasoning-token allocation, while a user may accept the default, customize the allocation, or exit. Larger allocations can improve accuracy but increase token cost and latency. We model this interaction as a Stackelberg game and derive the user's unique optimal customized allocation in closed form. For any price, the acceptable defaults form either an empty set or a compact interval. We characterize the provider's optimal default through a three-regime rule, reduce equilibrium computation to a one-dimensional price optimization, and prove the existence of the equilibrium. We further show that defaults affect the implemented reasoning allocation only when users value the convenience of avoiding customization; otherwise, every service-providing outcome implements the user's optimal customized allocation. Experiments with two compact open-weight reasoning models on five mathematics and science benchmarks support the accuracy-token model and show how model and task characteristics determine equilibrium prices, defaults, and reasoning allocations.

</details>

### [256] [ARIES-Mission2: A Zero-Shot Vision-Language-Action Framework for Fast Large-Scale Aerial Mission Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12763)
*Junhao Wei,Yanxiao Li,Haochen Li,Yifu Zhao,Dexing Yao,Baili Lu,Zikun Li,Yapeng Wang,Sio-Kei Im,Dingcheng Yang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出ARIES-Mission2，一个零样本视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，用于低空无人机任务生成。该框架将视觉语义感知与物理路径优化解耦，利用DeepSeek-V3进行任务解析、Molmo-7B进行目标定位，并将多目标遍历建模为旅行商问题（TSP）以优化路径。在UAV-VLPA-nano-30基准上，该方法将飞行距离从79.66 km缩短至62.43 km，比未优化的VLA基线降低21.6%，比人工规划降低9.5%，且任务耗时比人类专家快3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在语义理解上表现优异，但在低空无人机任务生成中直接使用受限于空间优化能力薄弱和路径规划效率低下，因此需要一个新的框架来弥合VLM语义理解与物理路径优化之间的鸿沟。

Method: ARIES-Mission2采用零样本VLA框架，将视觉语义感知与物理路径优化解耦。前端使用DeepSeek-V3进行自然语言任务解析、Molmo-7B进行零样本目标接地（grounding），再通过地理空间插值将像素坐标转换为GPS航点。后端将多目标无人机遍历建模为旅行商问题（TSP），比较原始VLM访问顺序与PSO、GPSO、IPSO三种优化路线，选择最小代价的闭环路线用于任务生成。

Result: 在UAV-VLPA-nano-30基准上，ARIES-Mission2实现总飞行距离62.43 km，相比未优化VLA基线（79.66 km）缩短21.6%，相比人工规划（69.00 km）缩短9.5%。完整30任务流程耗时575.40 s（平均19.18 s/任务），比人类专家规划快约3.6倍。VLM推理占主要耗时（19.02 s/任务），TSP求解仅需0.16 s/任务。可扩展性分析表明TSP模块在目标数增加时计算时间增长更平缓。

Conclusion: ARIES-Mission2通过解耦语义感知与物理优化，并引入TSP路由求解，有效解决了VLM在无人机路径规划中的空间优化弱点，显著提升了任务生成效率与路径质量，且具备良好的可扩展性。该框架验证了将VLM语义能力与传统组合优化方法相结合的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong semantic understanding capabilities, but their direct use in low-altitude Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) mission generation remains limited by weak spatial optimization and inefficient route planning. To address this issue, we propose ARIES-Mission2, a zero-shot Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework that decouples visual-semantic perception from physical route optimization. Given natural-language instructions and satellite imagery, ARIES-Mission2 first uses DeepSeek-V3 for task parsing and Molmo-7B for zero-shot target grounding, and then converts detected pixel locations into GPS waypoints through geospatial interpolation. To reduce the redundant backtracking caused by raw VLM-generated visiting orders, the back end formulates multi-target UAV traversal as a Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and compares four candidate routes, including the raw VLM order and the routes optimized by PSO, GPSO, and IPSO. The minimum-cost closed-loop route is then selected for mission generation. Experiments on the UAV-VLPA-nano-30 benchmark show that ARIES-Mission2 achieves a total flight distance of 62.43 km, reducing the route length by 21.6% compared with the unoptimized VLA baseline (79.66 km) and by 9.5% compared with manual human planning (69.00 km). The complete 30-task workflow takes 575.40 s, averaging 19.18 s per task, which is approximately 3.6 times faster than human expert planning. Component-level timing shows that VLM inference dominates the runtime with 19.02 s per task, while the TSP solver requires only 0.16 s per task. Scalability analysis further indicates that the TSP module maintains lower growth in computation time as the number of targets increases.

</details>


### [257] [Julia for CFD: A Critical Survey of Ecosystem, Performance, and Composability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12801)
*Tianbai Xiao*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文对Julia语言在CFD（计算流体力学）软件中的应用进行批判性综述，评估其在性能、可扩展性、加速器可移植性、自动微分和软件组合方面的能力与局限，指出Julia已超越概念验证阶段，但生态成熟度仍不及传统C/C++/Fortran环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代CFD日益嵌入设计、推理、优化和数据驱动建模的工作流中，需要连接物理模型、数值内核、异构硬件、微分和学习。Julia提供了独特的高层科学抽象、性能和组合能力，但缺乏系统性的证据评估其在CFD领域的实际优势与限度。

Method: 作者对代表性开源Julia CFD项目进行批判性调查，综合应用层面的证据，从性能、可扩展性、加速器可移植性、自动微分和软件组合五个维度进行分析，并与C++性能可移植框架、有限元DSL和基于JAX的可微CFD进行对比。

Result: 发布的证据表明Julia原生CFD在大型分布式CPU系统和多GPU平台上表现可信，并出现新兴的可微工作流。但与其他框架相比，这些能力并非Julia独有，其独特之处在于通过共享类型、分派和特化实现集成。证据喜忧参半：Julia在若干CFD领域已超越概念验证，但仍缺乏成熟生态、工业工具和部署经验。

Conclusion: Julia目前最强角色是作为开发和测试连接仿真与下游分析的CFD架构的平台，而非全面替代传统HPC CFD生态的成熟方案。

Abstract: Modern CFD increasingly places simulation inside workflows for design, inference, optimization, and data-driven modeling, creating pressure to connect physical models, numerical kernels, heterogeneous hardware, differentiation, and learning. Julia offers a distinctive approach: high-level scientific abstractions can be specialized for performance and composed within a common language and compiler ecosystem. This critical survey examines where that model benefits CFD software and where its limits remain. We review representative open-source projects and synthesize application-level evidence on performance, scalability, accelerator portability, automatic differentiation, and software composition. Published results demonstrate credible Julia-native CFD on large distributed CPU systems and multi-GPU platforms, as well as emerging differentiable workflows. Comparisons with C++ performance-portability frameworks, finite-element domain-specific languages, and JAX-based differentiable CFD show that these capabilities are not unique to Julia. Julia's distinction is their integration through shared types, dispatch, and specialization. The evidence is mixed: Julia has progressed beyond proof of concept in several CFD regimes, but still lacks the ecosystem breadth, industrial tooling, and deployment experience of established C/C++/Fortran environments. Its strongest current role is as a platform for developing and testing CFD architectures that connect simulation with downstream analysis.

</details>


### [258] [Spinal Coupling in Frontal and Transversal Plane During Gait - A Segmental and Time-Dependent Analysis of the Thoracic and Lumbar Spine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12945)
*Jonas Dully,Carlo Dindorf,Henriette Rönsch,Dennis Perchthaler,Jürgen Konradi,Ulrich Betz,Michael Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究利用非侵入性光栅立体摄影系统结合电动跑步机，在642名受试者中量化了步行过程中从T3到L4各椎体旋转与侧向偏移之间的耦合行为，并分析了静态矢状面姿势对该耦合的影响。结果发现了所有三个指标均呈头尾方向的分布模式，且静态矢状面姿势显著调节这些模式，为脊柱生物力学提供了首个动态、椎体层面的运动耦合特征描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱运动学中侧向偏移与轴向旋转之间的耦合是公认特征，但该耦合在步态中单个椎体水平的行为以及与矢状面姿势的关联尚不清楚，此前缺乏椎体级别的动态耦合量化研究。

Method: 采用非侵入性光栅立体摄影系统与仪器化跑步机，对642名受试者进行脊柱运动学分析。量化T3至L4每个椎体在步行中旋转和侧向偏移的时间依赖耦合，使用傅里叶级数推导的绝对相位滞后、归一化有符号面积和倾斜角三个指标进行分析，并评估静态矢状面姿势对耦合的影响。

Result: 三个指标均呈现出从头端到尾端的分布模式，几乎所有相邻椎体之间均存在差异，并在耦合行为发生翻转的不同转折点处表现不同。广义及汇总的静态矢状面姿势均显著调节这些模式，个体偏离静态矢状面姿势也会影响各指标。

Conclusion: 该研究首次提供了步态中椎体级别的动态脊柱耦合特征，为理解脊柱生物力学和静态矢状面姿势的影响奠定了基础，对脊柱疾病的诊断和治疗具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Introduction: Coupling between lateral deviation and axial rotation is a known feature of spinal mechanics, yet its behavior at the individual vertebral level during gait, as well as the association with sagittal posture remains poorly understood. Methods: This study analyzed spinal kinematics in a diverse cohort (n=642) using a non-invasive rasterstereography system with an instrumented treadmill, quantifying time-dependent coupling of rotation and lateral deviation for each vertebra from T3 to L4 during walking as well as the influence of sagittal posture on this coupling. Coupling behavior was analyzed with absolute phase lag, normalized signed area and tilt angle derived from the Fourier series. Results: Results revealed cranio-caudal patterns for all three metrics with differences between almost all adjacent vertebrae and different turning points, i.e., where the coupling behavior changed (from increase to decrease and vice versa). Generalized, as well as pooled static sagittal posture significantly modulated these patterns, while individual deviations from static sagittal posture influenced the metrics. Conclusion: These findings provide the first dynamic, vertebra-level characterization of spinal coupling during gait. Furthermore, they offer a foundation for an understanding of spinal biomechanics and the influence of static sagittal posture, potentially relevant to the diagnosis and treatment of spinal disorders.

</details>


### [259] [TIEM: Temporal Integration of Hypergraph Evidence and Skill Memory for Event-Driven Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13024)
*Wenjin Liu,Shen Pang,Tiesunlong Shen,Zhe Cui,Xiaobao Wu,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出TIEM，一个带时间戳门控的事件驱动催化剂结果预测框架，通过事件-证据超图、案例技能记忆和异构证据-经验融合推理三个组件解决训练数据污染和时间泄漏导致的"证据鸿沟"问题，并在五个金融预测基准上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于检索和记忆增强的大语言模型智能体在事件驱动催化剂结果预测中日益普及，但训练数据污染和时间泄漏会造成报告准确率与真实预测能力之间的"证据鸿沟"，需要一种能严格区分时态证据的方法。

Method: TIEM框架包含三个协同组件：1) 事件-证据超图(EEH)用于时间戳过滤的多级检索；2) 案例技能记忆(CSM)用于带来源标签的时序技能；3) 异构证据-经验融合推理(HEFR)用于证据-经验融合与预测。同时引入FinPURE（近期A股留出基准）和Name-Date探针来评估模型对名称-日期的敏感性。

Result: 在五个金融预测基准上的实验结果显示，TIEM优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: TIEM通过显式的时间戳门控机制有效缓解了训练数据污染和时间泄漏问题，为金融催化剂结果预测提供了更可靠的评估与预测框架。

Abstract: Event-driven catalyst-outcome forecasting increasingly uses retrieval- and memory-augmented large language model agents for prediction. However, training-data contamination and temporal leakage can create an Evidence Chasm between reported accuracy and true predictive ability. We propose TIEM, a timestamp-gated framework with three coordinated components: an Event-Evidence Hypergraph (EEH) for timestamp-filtered multi-tier retrieval; a Case-based Skill Memory (CSM) for source-tagged temporal skills; and Heterogeneous Evidence-Experience Fusion Reasoning (HEFR) for evidence-experience fusion and prediction. We also introduce FinPURE, a recent-period A-share holdout benchmark, and use a Name-Date Probe to assess per-model name-date sensitivity rather than assuming training cutoffs. Results on five financial forecasting benchmarks show TIEM outperforms current baselines. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/Fin_TIEM.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoKiFormer是一种新型大语言模型架构，通过引入局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决自注意力缺乏局部归纳偏置和MoE知识存储与计算耦合的问题，预训练收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练中存在两个主要低效问题：(i) 自注意力缺乏对局部信息的显式归纳偏置，导致序列内部局部信息被冗余建模；(ii) 混合专家模型（MoE）将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。这些问题限制了预训练的效率与效果。

Method: LoKiFormer在标准decoder架构上增强两个专用模块：1) 局部融合注意力（LFA），通过引入卷积融合至注意力机制，显式捕获局部模式，使注意力能够处理更具信息量的表示；2) 知识记忆模块（KMM），引入参数化键值记忆，将全局知识存储在可寻址的槽位中，解耦存储与计算，支持直接的知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练中的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，证明其在信息集成效率和有效性上优于现有LLM架构。

Conclusion: 通过LFA和KMM分别增强序列内部局部信息处理和序列外部全局知识访问，LoKiFormer实现了两个层面的高效信息集成，显著加速了预训练收敛，验证了其作为新型LLM架构的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [261] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量的坝体形变与卫星天气），构建并评估预测尼泊尔喜马拉雅冰湖溃决、降雨型滑坡及冰川小洪水风险的概率模型。在空间交叉验证与严格基线下，天气因子能有效预测触发时机（ROC 0.73-0.83），地形仅部分有效（真实值0.54-0.76），深度模型未显著优于梯度提升基线，最终给出尼泊尔排序观察清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前序可行性研究虽证明坝体形变能指示湖体失稳、天气能指示风险时机，但未提出可操作的预测模型。本文旨在填补这一空白，在仅用免费数据的前提下，分别回答'哪个地点易发'与'何时触发'两个问题，为高寒山区灾害预警提供低成本可推广的建模框架。

Method: 研究覆盖三类灾害（冰碛/冰坝溃决、降雨触发滑坡、冰川上及周边池塘的小洪水），每类灾害拆分两个独立问题（易发性与触发时机）。使用589个HMAGLOFDB年代溃决事件及数千条目录化滑坡记录，为每个事件匹配相似且未失稳的地点作为对照。所有模型在'整幅地图瓦片被扣留'的空间交叉验证下评估，硬性对照简单基线。天气用前期气象条件做触发时间预测（ROC），地形用多类模型做易发性排序，并报告朴素与配对对照两种分数。另测试五个深度学习模型与梯度提升基线及三规则决策树对比。

Result: 前期天气预测触发时机的ROC为：大型溃决0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82。地形排名：朴素评分接近0.9，但配对对照后为0.76（大溃决）、0.71（滑坡）、0.54（小洪水，接近随机）。溃决信号在尼泊尔单区达0.89。五种深度学习模型未决定性胜过梯度提升基线（仅滑坡上略高，证据不足）；湖灾场景基线直接胜出，可复现为基于崎岖度与季风降雨的三规则决策树。最终产出尼泊尔排序观察清单（作为优先级辅助而非预测）。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据足以对冰湖溃决等灾害进行有限的概率预警：天气是可靠的时间触发器，地形仅在部分场景（尤其尼泊尔溃决）提供有效空间排序，对小池塘洪水则地形失效。深度学习未带来突破，简单基线仍是最稳健选择。研究如实标注了免费数据的能力边界，强调输出为观察清单而非确定性预测。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [262] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH（Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History）架构，通过周期性缓存循环状态检查点作为状态锚点，并结合内容条件的锚点键实现注意力式聚合，在保持线性计算效率的同时扩展状态空间模型的记忆容量，解决了循环模型长上下文回忆能力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽然拥有随上下文长度增长的分词级记忆，但训练时具有二次方计算复杂度和推理时线性增长的KV缓存。循环替代方案虽然解码高效（固定大小状态压缩整个历史），但早期关联信息往往被后续更新覆盖，导致在回忆密集型任务上表现不佳。因此需要一种既能保持循环模型效率、又能有效扩展记忆容量的架构。

Method: MARCH周期性地将累积的循环状态检查点缓存为"状态锚点"，每个锚点关联一个紧凑的、内容条件化的锚点键。网络维持一个可随上下文长度增长的记忆库，提供历史分辨率与记忆成本之间的可控权衡。在每一时间步，MARCH生成一个锚点查询来关注所有因果可用的状态锚点，输出通过注意力风格的聚合方式在当前状态上对所有历史锚点进行加权求和。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文内检索等多个基准上持续优于多种线性注意力变体。

Conclusion: 内容路由的状态缓存能够显著增强循环模型的长程记忆能力，同时保留其固有的高效计算路径，为长上下文建模提供了一种兼具效率与回忆性能的折中方案。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [263] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VGG-MADiffRL（值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法）和MDCA（基于扩散的分层控制架构），用于多AUV自组网环境下的协同目标跟踪任务，通过扩散策略与值梯度引导结合，解决了现有MARL方法在高维联合状态-动作建模和噪声敏感策略生成方面的不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AUV自组网协同目标跟踪面临声学通信受限、动态拓扑和海洋扰动等挑战。现有的多智能体强化学习方法存在高维联合状态-动作空间建模困难以及策略生成对噪声敏感的问题，导致训练不稳定和跟踪性能退化，亟需新的方案来提升协同跟踪的稳定性和精度。

Method: 提出了VGG-MADiffRL算法和MDCA三级闭环分层控制架构。MDCA包含全局智能控制层、本地在线训练层和物理动作执行层，实现任务分配、局部决策与执行反馈的协同优化。VGG-MADiffRL基于扩散策略，在反向去噪过程中引入值梯度引导动作生成，使生成动作朝更高期望回报方向优化；同时采用双值网络联合优化与软目标更新策略，缓解过估计和训练振荡问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中实现了更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度以及更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和实际工程价值。

Conclusion: VGG-MADiffRL与MDCA架构能够有效解决多AUV协同目标跟踪中的训练不稳定和性能退化问题，为动态水下环境中的多AUV协同任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [264] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成建模统一表述为路径积分形式，其中流模型、扩散模型、变分模型和对抗模型均作为同一"主作用量"的不同评估原则。基于MSRJD形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，从而引入费曼图微扰理论。该展开提供了一阶环路修正，可在无需随机采样成本的情况下改善确定性采样器，在可解及非线性漂移上验证中将树级误差从53%降至1.6%。不完美的学习评分作为插入项，导出响应加权的评分匹配目标；对称等变漂移设计成为具有EFT幂次计数的算子展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成建模方法（流、扩散、变分、对抗）虽在形式上多样，但缺乏统一的数学框架来理解它们之间的内在联系与差异。同时，确定性采样器（如ODE-based sampler）存在系统性误差，而传统随机采样虽能改善但成本高昂，亟需一种既能统一理论视角又能降低采样误差与成本的新范式。

Method: 将生成建模表述为路径积分形式，构建单一"主作用量"（master action）。利用MSRJD形式体系将概率流分解为自由部分与相互作用部分，使系统可以应用费曼图微扰展开。具体包括：(1) 推导一阶单圈修正项，用于校正确定性采样器的系统误差而不引入额外随机采样；(2) 将不完美的学习评分视为微扰插入项，导出一个响应加权的评分匹配目标函数；(3) 将对称等变漂移设计转化为带EFT（有效场论）幂次计数的算子展开。

Result: 在可解模型与非线性漂移的数值验证中，所提出的一圈修正方案将树级（tree-level）误差从53%显著降至1.6%，展示了路径积分微扰方法对确定性采样器误差校正的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过MSRJD路径积分框架，生成建模的多种范式获得了统一的理论基础，并在此基础上发展出可计算的微扰修正方法，为生成模型的理论分析与算法改进提供了新的有力工具。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [265] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-AddGraph，一个针对动态图异常检测模型AddGraph的严格事后（post-hoc）可解释性框架。它通过双时空归因（DSTA）机制，分别从空间、短期时间（零额外成本）、长期时间三个维度提供边缘异常检测的解释，同时保持检测性能完全不变（Delta AUC=0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度动态图异常检测器虽已达到较强精度，但缺乏可解释性——当边缘被标记为异常时，分析人员只能得到分数而无法获得原因。这种不透明性在需要可审计和可信自动化决策的协同、受监管信息系统中是不可接受的。AddGraph作为基础的GCN+GRU动态图边缘级异常检测框架，从未配备任何可解释性机制。

Method: 提出X-AddGraph框架，构建于Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA)机制之上，包含三个与AddGraph架构模块一一对齐的组件：(1) 基于梯度的当前邻接结构相关性归因（空间维度）；(2) 直接读取推理期间已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时间维度，零额外成本）；(3) 通过循环隐藏状态的梯度回滚（长期时间维度）。检测器保持冻结状态。

Result: 检测性能被精确保持（Delta AUC=0，经验验证到小数点后十位）。在UCI Message基准上，训练的AddGraph基线达到每快照平均AUC 0.8705，超过原始发表结果；X-AddGraph逐分复现所有检测分数并为每个预测添加解释。在四类边缘群体（高置信真阳性、低置信真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）上，长期归因识别出携带显著更多反事实信号的历史快照（0.127 vs 0.074），这是空间盲解释器无法提供的能力。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph在完全不损害检测性能的前提下，为AddGraph赋予了多层次（空间、短期时间、长期时间）的可解释性，并在基准上验证了其有效性与实用性，代码已开源以支持全面复现。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [266] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的睡眠分期参考图谱（LBH）构建方法，利用多评分者数据集通过混淆矩阵建模每位评分者的分期行为，聚合概率生成更可靠的参考标签，在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上相比数据集图谱和最佳评分者图谱均有性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动睡眠分期研究大多仅以单一参考图谱为评估标准，忽略了评分者间的可变性。多评分者数据集中包含多个专家的集体判断信息，但尚未被有效利用来构建更可靠的参考标签。

Method: 从EEG（C3-M2）和下巴EMG中提取每30秒epoch共60个特征，提出基于学习的分期图谱（LBH），利用机器学习分类器的混淆矩阵建模每位评分者的各分期行为，列归一化后估计各真实睡眠分期在给定评分者标签下的概率，跨评分者聚合概率以分配最终分期标签。评估使用随机森林、支持向量机和多层感知器，在EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下与数据集图谱（DH）和最佳评分者图谱（BSH）对比。

Result: LBH在所有实验设置下均一致地提升了整体性能。最佳结果由随机森林与EEG+EMG组合取得：DOD-H上达到86.07%准确率、85.46%精确率和85.29% F1分数；DOD-O上达到86.04%准确率、85.21%精确率和84.70% F1分数。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模能够在不丢弃个体专家信息的情况下改进参考图谱的构建质量，多评分者数据集的集体行为可用于生成比单一参考图谱更可靠的睡眠分期标签。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [267] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练Transformer模型中存在一种"预测方向"特权锚点，残余流统计量围绕该锚点呈几何与行为上的分层结构，该现象在18种模型中均成立，揭示了高维计算与线性读出共存的新几何机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型会发展出特权基（privileged bases），但此前不清楚这些坐标轴究竟选择了何种方向。研究者希望探究被选中的方向的性质，特别是预测方向是否构成一个内容定义的锚点，以及残余流围绕该锚点的几何结构如何组织。

Method: 以预测token的反嵌入方向（unembedding direction）为锚，测量残余流中每个位置相对于该锚的统计特性。在18个模型（稠密与MoE、7B-120B、基础与指令微调）中进行几何分析、方差分析、因果干预（扰动靠近/远离预测方向的方向）和时序分析。

Result: 预测方向作为内容定义的特权锚，残余流按其接近预测的程度呈几何和行为分层。狭窄的尺度不变预测接口集中了读出相关结构，而远端补集随模型规模扩展；预测近端区域高度结构化并聚类相关提示，补集区域平坦且反区分。破坏最接近预测的方差方向立即导致发散和任务框架转换，次近方向延迟发散；补集每方向弱对齐但因果及时序上承重，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Conclusion: 预测方向构成了与先前描述的坐标轴不同的特权锚，为高维计算与线性读出如何共存提供了几何解释——窄而集中的读出接口与广阔的承重补集互补存在。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [268] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的客观分类方法，用于对飞行器在多种飞行姿态下由阵风引起的气动载荷进行归类。通过对3480个压力载荷测量数据（涉及六种飞行姿态）进行表征学习和汇总，识别出九种跨越多个姿态的重复出现的根本响应类型，这些类型可作为客观的分类准则，且比基于粗参数的标签更具可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在寻找一种客观的分类判据，以组织多种飞行条件下阵风诱导载荷的复杂性，同时其可解释性不逊于基于飞行姿态等粗参数的标签方法。传统基于粗参数的标签虽然直观但可能不够精细，需要更客观且可解释的分类方式。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心步骤：(1) 通过机器学习表示对大量实验观测进行编码；(2) 应用汇总（summarization）程序选择一组最小化的高显著性样本（exemplars）。这些样本为所有观测提供基于相似性的客观分类标准。

Result: 在包含3480个由随机阵风在飞翼模型六个飞行姿态下诱导的压力载荷测量数据上，该方法识别出九种跨多个姿态重复出现的根本响应类型。对特定类型的瞬态响应分析揭示了其背后的流体力学物理直觉。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于范例（exemplars）的相似性分类方法提供了一种客观且可解释的分类准则，能够跨越不同飞行姿态有效组织阵风载荷的复杂性，同时选出的范例便于专家深入检查和开展更精细的实验。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [269] [FlowLOB: Efficient and Controllable Limit Order Book Generation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13096)
*Zhuohan Wang,Andreea Bacalum,Ollie Olby,Carmine Ventre,Namid Stillman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowLOB是一个基于条件流匹配的订单簿轨迹生成器，在港交所多个股票代码上训练（0.1s、1s、10s三种采样频率），相比扩散模型和基线生成模型在真实感、采样效率和跨标的泛化方面表现更优，且支持反事实可控性场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的订单簿模拟器（基于代理的模型和深度生成模型）无法同时满足四个关键需求：真实的市场动态、计算高效的采样、可控的场景生成，以及超越训练时可见金融工具的泛化能力。需要一个在各方面都能兼顾的统一框架。

Method: 提出FlowLOB，采用条件流匹配（flow matching）框架生成订单簿轨迹。关键做法包括：(1) 在多个HKEX标的、三种采样频率下以tick相对表示训练；(2) 由于流和扩散模型具有统一表示，以完全相同的数据、架构和训练预算训练两者，并使用相同固定步长ODE求解器公平对比；(3) 通过分布检验评估反事实可控性（改变场景条件是否使生成统计量移向真实尾部区制）；(4) 在留出标的上进行zero-shot泛化测试，并做网络架构与学习率的消融实验。

Result: 流匹配仅需10步ODE求解即可达到最佳质量，而扩散模型需要更多函数评估才能接近相同保真度。在该高效运行点上，FlowLOB在两种较细采样频率下，在大多数分布指标上优于两种学习模型和两种基于代理的基线模型。反事实可控性测试中，FlowLOB在大多数设置下满足标准。真实感和控制效果均能zero-shot迁移到留出标的上。

Conclusion: FlowLOB证明了流匹配方法在订单簿模拟中的优势：在更少的采样步数下获得更高的真实感，同时具备可控场景生成和跨标的泛化能力，为高频金融模拟提供了一种兼具效率和质量的生成式框架。

Abstract: Limit order book (LOB) simulators are most useful to practitioners when they combine realistic market dynamics, computationally efficient sampling, controllable scenario generation, and the ability to generalize beyond the instruments seen during training---properties that existing agent-based and deep generative simulators provide only partially. We present \textbf{FlowLOB}, a conditional \textbf{flow}-matching generator of \textbf{LOB} trajectories, trained on multiple Hong Kong Exchange (HKEX) symbols at three sampling frequencies ($0.1$s, $1$s, $10$s) in tick-relative representation that transfers to unseen instruments. Because flow and diffusion models admit a common formulation, we train both with identical data, architecture, and budget, and sample both through the same fixed-step ODE solvers, yielding a controlled comparison of sampling efficiency and fidelity. Flow matching attains its best quality with only $10$ ODE-solver steps, whereas diffusion needs many more function evaluations to approach the same fidelity. At this efficient operating point, FlowLOB improves realism over baselines, two learned and two agent-based models, in most distributional metrics at the two finer sampling frequencies. We evaluate counterfactual controllability with a distributional test that asks whether changing a scenario condition moves the generated statistic toward the corresponding real tail regime; FlowLOB satisfies this criterion in most tested settings. Both realism and control effects transfer zero-shot on a held-out symbol. We additionally conduct ablation studies on the network architecture and the learning rate.

</details>


### [270] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG
